\title{Melanie-MASTERS (human masters) — Catalan}

\begin{document}
\maketitle

\begin{abstract}Every move below was produced by relational analogy to named master games (human and/or top-engine -- the exact source is named per move). Each figure shows our position with Melanie's move (a) and the top retrieved analog positions (b--d) with the master move played there; the caption names the exact source and the piece-role correspondence under $\mu$ -- cross-type maps (a rook playing a queen's role) are called out, as no material/eval engine explains a move that way.\end{abstract}

\caption{\textbf{Move 7, Black: \texttt{c6}.} (a) Our position; Melanie plays \texttt{c6} (\texttt{c7c6}). Panels (b)--(d) are the retrieved master positions whose correspondence most drove this move (highest QAP objective among those that transfer to it). (b) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0024\#2998} (ply 23) [structural distance $d$=77, QAP obj=112]: the master played \texttt{Bd7}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pc2} plays the role of that game's \emph{Bc1} (a \textbf{cross-type} correspondence); cross-type roles: our \emph{N}b8 as their \emph{P}b7, our \emph{P}e6 as their \emph{N}b4; transferred move \texttt{c7c6} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (c) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0019\#960} (ply 27) [structural distance $d$=57, QAP obj=106]: the master played \texttt{Nd7}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pc2} plays the role of that game's \emph{Nb3} (a \textbf{cross-type} correspondence); cross-type roles: our \emph{N}f6 as their \emph{R}f8, our \emph{N}b8 as their \emph{P}a7; transferred move \texttt{c7c6} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (d) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0019\#1851} (ply 23) [structural distance $d$=63, QAP obj=75]: the master played \texttt{Nc6}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pc2} plays the role of that game's \emph{Nb1} (a \textbf{cross-type} correspondence); cross-type roles: our \emph{B}c8 as their \emph{R}a8, our \emph{p}g3 as their \emph{B}c8; transferred move \texttt{c7c6} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. Synthesis: 3 retrieved analogs transferred to \texttt{c6} (summed QAP objective 293, the strongest correspondence among the candidate moves); Melanie committed it via the \texttt{search} selector.}
\label{fig:thought-m13}
\end{figure}
